## How this list is built

- Jobs come straight from employers' own hiring systems (Greenhouse, Lever, Ashby, Workday and others), collected by [JobsGlitch](https://jobsglitch.com).
- Only pay the employer wrote in the posting counts. Nothing here is estimated or converted.
- "Share of its open roles" is how many of the company's open postings show pay. A low share often means pay is posted only where a law requires it.
- A role counts only if it was still open on the employer's page at a check in the last 7 days.
- Job boards, staffing agencies and other middlemen are left out.
- Companies with at least three open roles showing pay are listed, grouped by the country of those roles.
- Role links go to the employer's own page. "Check my fit" opens the same job on JobsGlitch.
- Every company here is in [`companies.json`](companies.json), with up to three recent roles each.
- Found a mistake, or a company that should not be here? Open an issue.
